`weighted_conv(a, w, L)`:
```

```

```

```

    a=a/s; sc=fast_max_score(a)

Inside multiplicative loop for each mode:
```
base_alpha = 0.2
no_accept=0
for iter in range(100000):
   if time>time_limit+phase_time: break
   A = rfft(a,L); c=irfft(A*A)[:2n-1]; cmax=...
   exact_score=2*n*cmax
   update best exact
   if mode lp: val = lp_value from c; weights = c**(p-1)
   else: weights=softmax
   W=rfft(weights,L); C=irfft(conj(A)*W)[:n]
   # target ref:
   pos = C > 1e-300
   if count pos: ref = np.exp(np.mean(np.log(C[pos]))) maybe; else ref=1
   logr = np.log(np.maximum(C,1e-300)/ref)
   # clip logr
   logr = np.clip(logr, -8,8)
   # candidates
   tri_best=None; tri_val=inf
   alphas = [base_alpha, base_alpha*0.5, ...]
   for al in alphas:
       trial = a * np.exp(-al*logr)
       if use_sym: sym
       normalize
       val_trial, _ = compute objective (requires A_trial/c)
       if val_trial < tri_val: ...
   if tri_val < val_current*(1-1e-12):
       a=tri_best; base_alpha=min(1.0, base_alpha*1.1)
       no_accept=0
   else:
       base_alpha=max(0.01, base_alpha*0.7)
       no_accept+=1
       if no_accept>200: break phase
```

Helper:
```
def _next_fft_len(n):
    if n <=1: return 1
    # use np.fft.next_fast_len
    try: return int(np.fft.next_fast_len(n))
    except: return 1 << (n-1).bit_length()
```
`next_fast_len` might not exist in old numpy? It does. If not, pow2.

`def _autocorr(a, L=None):`
```
n=a.size
if L is None: L=_next_fft_len(2*n-1)
A=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)
return c[:2*n-1], A
```
But if we call A*A, for real A complex. Good. Normalize small negative? Not here.

`def _weighted_conv(a, A, w, L):` uses provided A:
```
W=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
return np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*W, L)[:a.size]
```
If A computed from current a. If we need for candidate with new a, compute A.

`def _autocorr_value(a,L,mode,p,beta):` returns val, cmax, c?
Maybe separate:
```
def _objective_from_c(c, mode,p,beta):
   c=np.maximum(c,0)
   if mode=='lp':
       pp=int(p)
       sp=np.sum(np.power(c, pp))
       return sp**(1.0/pp)
   else:
       cmax=float(c.max())
       z=beta*(c-cmax)
       z=np.where(z < -745, -745, z)
       s=np.exp(z).sum()
       return cmax + np.log(s)/beta
```
But for gradient weights, need weights.

`def _lp_weights(c,p): return np.power(np.maximum(c,0), p-1)` ensure p int.
`def _soft_weights(c,beta): cmax=c.max(); z=...; e=np.exp(z); w=e/e.sum(); return w, cmax`

`def _log_to_a(x, n, use_sym):`
```
if x.size != expected; return None
z = x - np.mean(x)
# to avoid exp(>709), clip z to [-60,60]

    rem=...
    # lbfgs remaining 30%

       update
    rem...
    # coord 20%
    t_c=min(rem*0.8, 150)
    if t_c>10:
       a_c=coordinate_polish(best_a,t_c,use_sym)
       update
    return best_a,best_score
```

```
def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    f=np.asarray(f,float); f=np.maximum(f,0); s=f.sum(); if s<eps return ones(new_n)/new_n
    f=f/s
    old_x=np.linspace(0,1,len(f)); new_x=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
    g=np.interp(new_x, old_x, f); g=np.maximum(g,0); gs=g.sum(); if gs<eps return ones; return g/gs
```

def lbfgs_pipeline(a, time_limit, use_sym):
   n=len(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_score=fastexact
   # choose modes based on time
   if time_limit > 100:
      modes = [('lp',4,0,0.25), ('lp',8,0,0.25), ('soft',0,1e6,0.25), ('soft',0,5e6,0.25)]
   elif time_limit > 40:
      modes = [('lp',8,0,0.4), ('soft',0,2e6,0.6)]
   else:
      modes = [('soft',0,2e6,1.0)]
   for mode,p,beta,fr in modes:
       t_mode=fr*time_limit
       if t_mode < 15: continue
       a_out,sc=run_lbfgs(best_a, t_mode*0.9, use_sym, mode,p,beta,best_a,best_score)
       if sc<best_score: best=...
       # optional coordinate?
   return best
```

`_normalize_nonneg(a)`:
```
def _normalize_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0: return None
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15: return None
    a = a/s
    return a
```

`_objective_from_c(c, mode, p, beta)`:
```
if c is None: return inf
cmax=float(c.max())
if mode=='lp':
   pp=int(p)
   if pp <=1: return cmax
   powers=np.power(c, pp)
   S=float(np.sum(powers))
   if S <=0: return 0.0
   return float(S ** (1.0/pp))
elif mode=='soft':
   if cmax <=0: return 0.0
   b=float(beta)
   z=b*(c-cmax)
   z=np.where(z < -745.0, -745.0, z)
   e=np.exp(z)
   s=float(np.sum(e))
   if s <=0: return cmax
   return float(cmax + np.log(s)/b)
else: # top
   return cmax
```
For soft with beta huge, c-cmax up to 0, min -0.0002? z maybe -200; exp underflow to 0 except max. sum exp at max=1. val=cmax. Good.

`_weights_from_c(c, mode, p, beta, use_sym)`:
```
n_c=len(c)
if mode=='lp':
   pp=int(p)
   weights = np.power(c, pp-1) if pp>1 else np.ones(n_c)
   S = float(np.dot(weights, c))
elif mode=='soft':
   cmax=c.max(); z=...; e=...; s=e.sum(); weights=e/s; S=float(np.dot(weights,c))
else:
   K=int(p)
   if K <=0: K=64
   if K >= n_c: idx=np.arange(n_c)
   else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
   weights=np.zeros(n_c)
   weights[idx] = 1.0/len(idx)
   S = float(np.dot(weights,c))
if not use_sym:
   return weights, S
weights=0.5*(weights+weights[::-1])
# S unchanged (sym weights same dot if c sym)
return weights, S
```
If top K selects one of symmetric equal lags, sym weights pairs. Good.

`_log_to_a`:
```
def _log_to_a(x,n,use_sym):
   x=np.asarray(x,float)
   if use_sym:
      expected=(n+1)//2
   else: expected=n
   if x.size != expected: return None
   z=x-x.mean()
   z=np.clip(z, -60.0, 60.0)
   raw=np.exp(z)
   if use_sym:
      a=np.empty(n,float)
      if n%2==0:
         h=n//2
         a[:h]=raw; a[h:]=raw[::-1]
      else:
         h=(n+1)//2
         a[:h]=raw; a[h:]=raw[:h-1][::-1]
   else:
      a=raw.astype(np.float64)
   s=a.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-15: return None
   a=a/s
   # floor? maybe set min floor and renorm? To keep positive for gradient, use floor 1e-25
   a=np.maximum(a, 1e-30)
   s2=a.sum(); if s2<=...; return a/s2
```
If we floor after normalization, sum changes tiny. good. But in LBFGS, floor makes function not exactly invariant? negligible.

`_a_to_log`:
```
def _a_to_log(a,use_sym):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=a.size
   if use_sym:
      h=(n+1)//2
      half=a[:h]
   else: half=a
   floor=1e-25
   x=np.log(np.maximum(half, floor))
   x=x-x.mean()
   x=np.clip(x, -60.0, 60.0)
   return x
```
If use_sym false, length n. For sym, if a not sym, uses first half; start sym anyway.

`_log_fun_grad`:
```
def _log_fun_grad(x, n, use_sym, mode, p, beta, L):
    global _LBFGS_BEST_A, _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE
    a=_log_to_a(x,n,use_sym)
    if a is None:
       return (1e300, np.zeros_like(x))
    c,A=_autocorr_sym(a,L)
    exact=float(2.0*n*c.max())
    if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE:
       _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE=exact; _LBFGS_BEST_A=a.copy()
    if mode == 'lp':
        pp=int(p)
        if pp <=1:
           val=float(c.max()); weights=np.ones_like(c)
           factor=1.0
        else:
           powers=np.power(c, pp)
           S=float(np.sum(powers))
           if S <=0 or not np.isfinite(S): return (1e300, np.zeros_like(x))
           val=float(S ** (1.0/pp))
           weights=np.power(c, pp-1)
           factor=val / S
        # for p=1, grad? just max? not use.
    else: # soft
        cmax=float(c.max())
        if cmax <=0: val=0; weights=np.ones_like(c)/len(c); factor=1
        else:
            b=float(beta)
            z=b*(c-cmax)
            z=np.where(z < -745.0, -745.0, z)
            e=np.exp(z); s=float(np.sum(e))
            if s <=0: return (cmax, np.zeros_like(x))
            weights=e/s
            val=float(cmax + np.log(s)/b)
            factor=1.0
    if mode == 'lp' and pp <=1:
       # fallback gradient not used; approximate top weights? Set zeros.
       W=np.fft.rfft(weights,L); C=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*W,L)[:n]
       grad_a=2.0*factor*C
    else:
       W=np.fft.rfft(weights,L)
       C=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*W,L)[:n]
       if mode == 'lp':
          grad_a = 2.0 * factor * C
       else:
          grad_a = 2.0 * C
    # project
    mu=float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    gfull = a * (grad_a - mu)
    if not use_sym:
       gx = gfull
    else:
       h=(n+1)//2
       gx=np.zeros(h,float)
       if n%2==0:
          gx[:] = gfull[:n//2] + gfull[n-1:n-1-n//2:-1]
       else:
          hx=h-1
          if hx>0: gx[:hx] = gfull[:hx] + gfull[n-1:n-hx:-1]
          gx[hx] = gfull[hx]
    gx=np.nan_to_num(gx, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return (float(val), gx)
```
For Lp p=1 not used. Need define pp before if; set pp=1 if mode soft? Or handle. In code, if mode lp, define pp. Else skip.

For Lp p=2, factor=val/S. Grad =2factorC. Good.

For soft, weights sum1, val=dot weights c. Grad=2C. mu=dot(a,2C)=2val. gfull = a(2C-2val). Good.

For Lp, mu=2val. gfull = a(2factor C -2val). KKT target C=val/factor=S. Good.

`np.nan_to_num` available.

Potential issue: `_log_fun_grad` returns gradient with `use_sym`, but minimize x0 length half. In `_a_to_log` start length matches. Good.

def run_lbfgs(a, time_limit, use_sym, mode, p, beta, best_a_in, best_score_in):
    global _LBFGS_BEST_A, _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE
    a_in=_normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a_in is None: return best_a_in,best_score_in
    if use_sym: a_in = 0.5*(a_in+a_in[::-1]); a_in=a_in/sum
    n=a_in.size; L=_next_fft_len(2*n-1)

    # reset global to best in
    _LBFGS_BEST_A=best_a_in.copy()
    _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE=float(best_score_in)
    # timing call

       _log_fun_grad(x0,n,use_sym,mode,p,beta,L)
    except Exception:
       pass
    eval_time=max(1e-6, time.monotonic()-t0)
    # choose maxfun: reserve 5s? maxfun = int((time_limit-5)/(4*eval_time))
    maxfun = int((time_limit - 5.0) / (4.0 * eval_time))
    maxfun = max(80, min(1500, maxfun))
    maxiter = max(20, min(300, maxfun//2))
    bounds=[(-60.0,60.0)] for _ in range(x0.size)]  # no lambda! Need list comprehension is allowed? Lambda not, list comp okay, but to be safe use loop.
```

       res = minimize(_log_fun_grad, x0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                      args=(n, use_sym, mode, p, beta, L),
                      bounds=bounds,
                      options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':20})

`lbfgs_pipeline`:

def lbfgs_pipeline(a, time_limit, use_sym):
    a=_normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a is None: return None,inf
    if use_sym: a=sym...
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_fast_exact_score(a)
    n=a.size
    # if n > 10000 maybe limit
    if time_limit <= 0: return
    if n <= 9000:
       if time_limit > 150:
          modes=[('lp',4,0.0,0.2),('lp',8,0.0,0.2),('soft',0.0,1e6,0.3),('soft',0.0,5e6,0.3)]
       elif time_limit > 60:
          modes=[('lp',8,0.0,0.4),('soft',0.0,2e6,0.6)]
       else:
          modes=[('soft',0.0,2e6,1.0)]
    else:
       modes=[('soft',0.0,2e6,1.0)]
    total=0
    for (mode,p,beta,fr) in modes:
       tmode=fr*time_limit
       if tmode < 15: continue
       # start from best_a; maybe if mode soft high, start from best
       a_out,sc=run_lbfgs(best_a, min(tmode-2, time_limit-total-1), use_sym, mode,p,beta,best_a,best_score)
       if a_out is not None and sc < best_score:
          best_a=a_out.copy(); best_score=sc
       total += tmode
       if total >= time_limit: break
    return best_a,best_score
```
Time allocations with fr sum >1? We multiply each by time_limit but not actual remaining? Use fraction of original time_limit. The loop may exceed if tmode sum > time_limit? sum fr=1 for first. Good. But we reserve? use `min(tmode, remaining)`.

def multiplicative_refine(a, time_limit, use_sym):
    a=_normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a is None: return None,inf
    if use_sym: a=_symmetrize_seq(a); a=_normalize_nonng(a)
    n=a.size; L=_next_fft_len(2*n-1)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_fast_exact_score(a)
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    modes=[...]
    # normalize weights sum
    # total weights
    def? no nested. Manual compute sum_weights = sum(fr)
    for mode,p,beta,fr in modes:
       phase_time = fr * time_limit
       # but if already used total, cap phase_time = min(phase_time, time_limit - elapsed)
       # We need track elapsed overall; since phase_time proportional orig, use phase_end = t_start + fr*time_limit? If sum fr=1, sequential okay. But if previous took longer due no break? We'll use:
       phase_limit = (fr * time_limit)
       # cap remaining
       remaining = time_limit - (time.monotonic()-t_start)
       t_phase = min(phase_limit, remaining)
       if t_phase < 5: continue
       phase_end = time.monotonic() + t_phase
       base_alpha = 0.14 if mode=='lp' else (0.07 if mode=='soft' else 0.10)
       no_acc=0
       # parent use local current a (not best)
       cur=a.copy()
       while time.monotonic() < phase_end:
          c,A=_autocorr_sym(cur,L)
          exact=2*n*c.max(); update best
          val_current=_objective_from_c(c,mode,p,beta)
          if not finite: break
          weights,S=_weights_from_c(c,mode,p,beta,use_sym)
          if S <= 1e-300: break
          W=np.fft.rfft(weights,L)
          C=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*W,L)[:n]
          C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
          ref=S
          logr=np.log(np.maximum(C,1e-300)/ref)
          logr=np.clip(logr, -4.0, 4.0)
          alphas=[base_alpha, base_alpha*0.5, base_alpha*0.25, base_alpha*0.125]
          best_trial=None; best_val=val_current; best_exact_trial=exact?;
          for al in alphas:
             trial = cur * np.exp(-al*logr)
             if use_sym: trial=0.5*(trial+trial[::-1])
             trial=_normalize_nonneg(trial)
             if trial is None: continue
             c2=A2? _c_from_a(trial,L)
             val2=_objective_from_c(c2,mode,p,beta)
             if not finite: continue
             # choose candidate with lower val2; if tie, lower exact
             exact2=2*n*c2.max()
             if val2 < best_val - 1e-18 or (abs(val2-best_val)<=1e-18 and exact2 < best_exact_trial? need store)
                 best_trial=trial; best_val=val2; best_exact_trial=exact2
          if best_trial is not None:
             cur=best_trial
             base_alpha=min(1.0, base_alpha*1.08)
             no_acc=0
          else:
             base_alpha=max(0.01, base_alpha*0.7)
             no_acc += 1
             if no_acc > 250: break
       # after mode, decide cur vs best? Use whichever has lower exact?
       # If cur exact better, update; else keep cur for next mode? Could set cur=best_a if best exact significantly better.
       sc_cur=_fast_exact_score(cur)
       if sc_cur < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_a=cur.copy(); best_score=sc_cur
       # To avoid bad drift, if cur exact much worse than best, reset cur=best_a
       if sc_cur > best_score * (1 + 1e-3):
           cur = best_a.copy()
       else:
           cur = cur.copy()
       a = cur
    return best_a,best_score
```

def coordinate_polish(a, time_limit, use_sym):
   a=_normalize_nonneg(a); if None return
   if use_sym: sym normalize
   n=a.size; L=...
   best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_fast_exact_score(a)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(2024)
   stall=0
   t_start=time.monotonic()
   # Determine source/dest counts based on n
   src_count=8; dst_count=5; frac=(0.05,0.12,0.25,0.5)
   while time.monotonic()-t_start < time_limit and stall < 80:
      c,A=_autocorr_sym(a,L)
      M=c.max(); exact=2nM; update
      # top weights K=32
      K=min(32, len(c))
      idx=np.argpartition(c,-K)[-K:]
      w=np.zeros(len(c)); w[idx]=1.0/K
      if use_sym: w=0.5*(w+w[::-1])
      W=np.fft.rfft(w,L); C=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*W,L)[:n]
      grad=2*C
      mu=float(np.dot(a,grad))
      # influence scores:
      src_score=a*grad  # moving all mass from i? Actually if move fraction f, change ~f a_i (grad_j - grad_i), so source high a_i grad_i, dest low a_j grad_j.
      # maybe use src_score = np.where(grad>0, src_score, -inf)
      src_idx=np.argpartition(-src_score, -src_count)[-src_count:] if src_count<n else np.arange(n)
      # dest low:
      dest_low=np.argpartition(np.concatenate? no)
      # Use dest_score = a*grad + 0.1*src? lower better.
      d_scores = src_score  # dest low if low score
      # exclude same as src and maybe mirror
      d_all=np.argsort(d_scores)[:dst_count*3]
      # build pairs
      pairs=[]
      for s in src_idx:
         for d in d_all:
            if d==s: continue
            if use_sym and (d == n-1-s): continue
            reduc = a[s]*grad[s] - a[d]*grad[d]
            if reduc > 0: pairs.append((reduc,s,d))
      pairs.sort(reverse=True)
      attempted=False
      for _,s,d in pairs[:5]:
         if time... break
         for f in frac:
            trial=a.copy()
            amt=a[s]*f
            if amt <=0 or amt > trial[s]: continue
            trial[s]-=amt; trial[d]+=amt
            if use_sym: trial=0.5*(trial+trial[::-1])
            trial=_normalize_nonneg(trial)
            if trial is None: continue
            sc=_fast_exact_score(trial)
            if sc < best_score - 1e-14:
               a=trial; best_a=trial.copy(); best_score=sc; stall=0; attempted=True; break
            # also exact_trial from c? fast uses FFT; okay
         if attempted: break
      if not attempted:
         stall+=1
         # random small moves
         for _ in range(3):
             s=int(rng.integers(n)); d=int(rng.integers(n))
             if s==d: continue
             f=0.02
             trial=a.copy(); amt=a[s]*f; if amt>0: ... evaluate
             if improves: ...
         if time per iteration too high? no.
   return best_a,best_score

Detailed `coordinate_polish` active:
```
def coordinate_polish(a, time_limit, use_sym):
   ...
   while time...:
      c,A? just c via _c_from_a (no A needed for active)
      M=c.max(); exact=...
      K=min(48, len(c))
      acts=np.argpartition(c,-K)[-K:]
      # weights maybe equal for pot
      pot=np.zeros(n)
      # also contrib? pot computed
      for k in acts:
         k=int(k)
         start=max(0,k-n+1); end=min(n-1,k)
         if start>end: continue
         idx=np.arange(start,end+1)
         j=k-idx
         pot[idx] += a[j]
         pot[j] += a[idx]
      score = a*pot
      # source candidates high score; dest low, but if dest has zero maybe low.
      src_idx=np.argpartition(-score, -10)[-10:]
      # dest: low score, but adding to low a? If a_j zero, moving mass in may be okay; score=0. Use low.
      cand_all=np.argsort(score)[:30]
      pairs=[]
      for s in src_idx:
         ss=a[s]
         if ss<=1e-12: continue
         for d in cand_all:
            if d==s: continue
            if use_sym and d == n-1-s: continue
            red = score[s] - score[d]
            if red > 0:
               pairs.append((red,s,d))
      pairs.sort(reverse=True)
      # also consider moving in opposite direction for best pairs? red negative maybe if d high, s low; already source high/dest low covers by pair orientation. Could also if score[d] > score[s], swap? But we choose top source and low dest globally.
      best_pair=None; best_active= np.inf; best_frac=0
      fracs=(0.01,0.03,0.06,0.12,0.25,0.5)
      for _,s,d in pairs[:12]:
         # for each fraction, compute active max
         for f in fracs:
            delta=a[s]*f
            if delta<=0: continue
            # compute active values efficiently:
            # base_vals = c[acts] copy
            # For each k in acts:
            # a_sk = a[s? k-d?] Wait a_{k-d} and a_{k-s}.
            active_vals = c[acts]
            for pos,k in enumerate(acts):
               vi = a[k-d] if 0 <= k-d < n else 0.0
               u_i = a[k-s] if 0 <= k-s < n else 0.0
               lin = 2.0*delta*(vi - u_i)
               quad = delta*delta
               if 2*d == k: quad += delta*delta
               # formula: delta^2*(I2d + I2s -2I s+d). We have base quad 0? For moving s->d, e^TR e = I2d + I2s -2I s+d. So:
               q=0.0
               if 2*d == k: q += delta*delta
               if 2*s == k: q += delta*delta
               if s+d == k: q -= 2.0*delta*delta
               active_vals[pos] += lin + q
            am=active_vals.max()
            if am < best_active:
               best_active=am; best_pair=(s,d,f)
      # Also maybe consider δ negative (move d->s) for pairs with score[s]-score[d] negative? We can loop top low->high? Not needed.
      # Additionally, allow source not in src_idx but if active_vals great? Could sample random 5 pairs? skip.
      # Verify top best candidate(s): exact FFT for best_pair and maybe some other candidates with active_max close to best. To save, just exact best.
      if best_pair is not None:
          s,d,f=best_pair; trial=a.copy(); delta=a[s]*f; if delta>a[s]: delta=a[s]; trial[s]-=delta; trial[d]+=delta
          if use_sym: sym
          trial=norm
          sc=fastexact
          if sc < best_score - 1e-14:
             a=trial; best...
             continue
      # If no exact improvement, maybe try a few candidates with active_max < best_score/(2n)+margin? Could exact-verify up to 3 with next best active.
```

def _noisy_smooth_start(base, n, seed, sigma=0.1):
    base=interpolate_sequence(base,n)
    base=_symmetrize_seq(base); base=normalize
    y=np.log(np.maximum(base,1e-25)); y-=y.mean()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)

    L=_next_fft_len(n)
    F=np.fft.rfft(noise,L)

```python
"""
Minimize 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2 using:
1. Parent construction from height_sequence_1 plus diverse initialized starts.
2. Adaptive peak-weighted multiplicative flattening (Lp / softmax / active-top).
3. Log-domain L-BFGS-B on smooth Lp and softmax-max objectives.
4. Active-peak coordinate local search with exact FFT verification.
5. Multi-length interpolation and full/symmetric exploration.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None

_LBFGS_BEST_A = None
_LBFGS_BEST_SCORE = float("inf")


def _next_fft_len(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    try:
        return int(np.fft.next_fast_len(m))
    except Exception:
        return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return None
    a = a / s
    return a


def _symmetrize_seq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    return 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])


def _autocorr_sym(a, L=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if L is None:
        L = _next_fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[: (2 * n - 1)]
    c = 0.5 * (c + c[::-1])
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return c, A


def _c_from_a(a, L):
    n = int(np.asarray(a).size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[: (2 * n - 1)]
    c = 0.5 * (c + c[::-1])
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _fast_exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = _next_fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    c, _ = _autocorr_sym(a, L)
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))


def _objective_from_c(c, mode, p, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if mode == "lp":
        pp = int(p)
        if pp <= 1:
            return cmax
        po = np.power(np.maximum(c, 0.0), pp)
        S = float(np.sum(po))
        if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
            return 1e300
        return float(S ** (1.0 / pp))
    if mode == "soft":
        b = float(beta)
        if cmax <= 0.0 or b <= 0.0:
            return cmax
        z = b * (c - cmax)
        z = np.where(z < -700.0, -700.0, z)
        e = np.exp(z)
        s = float(np.sum(e))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            return cmax
        return float(cmax + np.log(s) / b)
    # top / exact max
    return cmax


def _weights_from_c(c, mode, p, beta, use_sym):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(c.size)
    if mode == "lp":
        pp = int(p)
        if pp <= 1:
            w = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            w = np.power(np.maximum(c, 0.0), pp - 1)
        S = float(np.dot(w, c))
        if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-300:
            w = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
            S = 1.0
    elif mode == "soft":
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        b = float(beta)
        if cmax <= 0.0 or b <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / m
            S = 1.0
        else:
            z = b * (c - cmax)
            z = np.where(z < -700.0, -700.0, z)
            e = np.exp(z)
            s = float(np.sum(e))
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
                w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
                S = cmax
            else:
                w = e / s
                S = float(np.dot(w, c))
    else:
        K = int(p)
        if K <= 0:
            K = 128
        K = max(1, min(K, m))
        if K >= m:
            idx = np.arange(m)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, m - K)[m - K:]
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
        S = float(np.dot(w, c))
        if S <= 1e-300:
            S = 1e-300
    if use_sym:
        w = 0.5 * (w + w[::-1])
    return w, S


def _update_best(best_seq, best_score, a, label=""):
    if a is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    sc = _fast_exact_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        if label:
            print("*** NEW BEST [" + label + "] " + repr(sc) + " n=" + str(a.size))
        return a.copy(), sc
    return best_seq, best_score


def _interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    f = _normalize_nonneg(f)
    if f is None:
        return np.ones(int(new_n), dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    old_n = int(f.size)
    new_n = int(max(2, new_n))
    if old_n == new_n:
        return f.copy()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    g = np.interp(new_x, old_x, f)
    g = _normalize_nonneg(g)
    if g is None:
        g = np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    return g


def _default_sequence(n=6700):
    n = int(max(100, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    cands = [
        np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
        np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2,
        np.maximum(1.0 + 0.25 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x), 0.01),
        np.maximum(1.0 + 0.50 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x), 0.01),
        np.exp(-6.0 * x) + np.exp(6.0 * (x - 1.0)),
    ]
    best = None
    bst = float("inf")
    for a in cands:
        a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
        if a is None:
            continue
        s = _fast_exact_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < bst:
            bst = s
            best = a.copy()
    if best is None:
        best = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return best


def _noisy_smooth_start(base, n, seed, sigma=0.10):
    base = _interpolate_sequence(base, n)
    base = _symmetrize_seq(base)
    base = _normalize_nonneg(base)
    if base is None:
        return _default_sequence(n)
    n = int(base.size)
    y = np.log(np.maximum(base, 1e-25))
    y = y - float(y.mean())
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), n)
    L = _next_fft_len(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(noise, L)
    keep = max(1, F.size // 12)
    F[keep:] = 0.0
    noise = np.fft.irfft(F, L)[:n]
    y2 = y + noise
    y2 = y2 - float(y2.mean())
    y2 = np.clip(y2, -30.0, 30.0)
    a = np.exp(y2)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a is None:
        return base.copy()
    a = _symmetrize_seq(a)
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    return a


def _log_to_a(x, n, use_sym):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=60.0, neginf=-60.0)
    expected = (int(n) + 1) // 2 if use_sym else int(n)
    if x.size != expected:
        return None
    z = x - float(x.mean())
    z = np.clip(z, -60.0, 60.0)
    raw = np.exp(z)
    if use_sym:
        a = np.empty(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
        if int(n) % 2 == 0:
            h = int(n) // 2
            a[:h] = raw
            a[h:] = raw[::-1]
        else:
            h = (int(n) + 1) // 2
            a[:h] = raw
            a[h:] = raw[: h - 1][::-1]
    else:
        a = raw.astype(np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-30)
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    return a


def _a_to_log(a, use_sym):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if use_sym:
        h = (n + 1) // 2
        half = a[:h]
    else:
        half = a
    x = np.log(np.maximum(half, 1e-25))
    x = x - float(x.mean())
    x = np.clip(x, -60.0, 60.0)
    return x


def _log_fun_grad(x, n, use_sym, mode, p, beta, L):
    global _LBFGS_BEST_A, _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = _log_to_a(x, int(n), use_sym)
    if a is None:
        return 1e300, np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)

    c, A = _autocorr_sym(a, L)
    exact = float(2.0 * int(n) * np.max(c))
    if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE:
        _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE = exact
        _LBFGS_BEST_A = a.copy()

    if mode == "lp":
        pp = int(max(2, p))
        po = np.power(np.maximum(c, 0.0), pp)
        S = float(np.sum(po))
        if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
            return 1e300, np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
        val = float(S ** (1.0 / pp))
        weights = np.power(np.maximum(c, 0.0), pp - 1)
        factor = val / S
    else:
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        b = float(beta)
        if cmax <= 0.0 or b <= 0.0:
            return cmax, np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
        z = b * (c - cmax)
        z = np.where(z < -700.0, -700.0, z)
        e = np.exp(z)
        s = float(np.sum(e))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            return cmax, np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
        weights = e / s
        val = float(cmax + np.log(s) / b)
        factor = 1.0

    W = np.fft.rfft(weights, L)
    C = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A) * W, L)[: int(n)]
    C = np.nan_to_num(C, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    if mode == "lp":
        grad_a = 2.0 * factor * C
    else:
        grad_a = 2.0 * C

    mu = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    gfull = a * (grad_a - mu)
    gfull = np.nan_to_num(gfull, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    if not use_sym:
        gx = gfull.copy()
    else:
        h = (int(n) + 1) // 2
        gx = np.zeros(h, dtype=np.float64)
        if int(n) % 2 == 0:
            gx[:] = gfull[: n // 2] + gfull[n - 1 : n - 1 - n // 2 : -1]
        else:
            hx = h - 1
            if hx > 0:
                gx[:hx] = gfull[:hx] + gfull[n - 1 : n - hx : -1]
            gx[hx] = gfull[hx]

    return float(val), gx


def run_lbfgs(a, time_limit, use_sym, mode, p, beta, best_a_in, best_score_in):
    global _LBFGS_BEST_A, _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a is None or time_limit < 5.0:
        return best_a_in, best_score_in
    if use_sym:
        a = _normalize_nonneg(_symmetrize_seq(a))
        if a is None:
            return best_a_in, best_score_in

    n = int(a.size)
    L = _next_fft_len(2 * n - 1)

    _LBFGS_BEST_A = np.asarray(best_a_in, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE = float(best_score_in)

    x0 = _a_to_log(a, use_sym)

    if _scipy_minimize is None:
        return _LBFGS_BEST_A.copy(), float(_LBFGS_BEST_SCORE)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        _log_fun_grad(x0, n, use_sym, mode, p, beta, L)
    except Exception:
        pass
    eval_time = max(1e-6, time.monotonic() - t0)

    maxfun = int((float(time_limit) - 5.0) / (4.0 * eval_time))
    maxfun = max(80, min(1500, maxfun))
    maxiter = max(20, min(300, maxfun // 2))
    bounds = [(-60.0, 60.0)] * int(x0.size)

    try:
        res = _scipy_minimize(
            _log_fun_grad,
            x0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            args=(n, use_sym, mode, p, beta, L),
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "maxls": 20,
            },
        )
        xs = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        xs = x0

    a_res = _log_to_a(xs, n, use_sym)
    if a_res is not None:
        sc = _fast_exact_score(a_res)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE:
            _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE = sc
            _LBFGS_BEST_A = a_res.copy()

    if _LBFGS_BEST_A is None:
        return best_a_in, best_score_in
    return _LBFGS_BEST_A.copy(), float(_LBFGS_BEST_SCORE)


def lbfgs_pipeline(a, time_limit, use_sym):
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a is None or time_limit < 10.0:
        return None, float("inf")
    if use_sym:
        a = _normalize_nonneg(_symmetrize_seq(a))
        if a is None:
            return None, float("inf")

    n = int(a.size)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _fast_exact_score(a)
    if n > 9500:
        return best_a, best_score

    if time_limit > 160.0:
        modes = [
            ("lp", 4, 0.0, 0.20),
            ("lp", 8, 0.0, 0.20),
            ("soft", 0.0, 1.0e6, 0.30),
            ("soft", 0.0, 5.0e6, 0.30),
        ]
    elif time_limit > 60.0:
        modes = [
            ("lp", 8, 0.0, 0.40),
            ("soft", 0.0, 2.0e6, 0.60),
        ]
    else:
        modes = [("soft", 0.0, 2.0e6, 1.0)]

    elapsed = 0.0
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    for mode, p, beta, fr in modes:
        if time.monotonic() - t_start > time_limit - 5.0:
            break
        tmode = float(fr) * float(time_limit)
        remaining = float(time_limit) - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
        if tmode < 15.0 or remaining < 15.0:
            continue
        tmode = min(tmode, remaining - 2.0)
        if tmode < 10.0:
            continue
        a_out, sc_out = run_lbfgs(
            best_a, tmode, use_sym, mode, int(p), float(beta), best_a, best_score
        )
        if a_out is not None and np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_score:
            best_a = a_out.copy()
            best_score = float(sc_out)
        elapsed += tmode
    return best_a, best_score


def multiplicative_refine(a, time_limit, use_sym):
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    if use_sym:
        a = _normalize_nonneg(_symmetrize_seq(a))
        if a is None:
            return None, float("inf")

    n = int(a.size)
    L = _next_fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _fast_exact_score(a)

    modes = [
        ("lp", 4, 0.0, 0.08),
        ("lp", 6, 0.0, 0.10),
        ("lp", 8, 0.0, 0.12),
        ("soft", 0.0, 1.0e5, 0.10),
        ("soft", 0.0, 5.0e5, 0.10),
        ("soft", 0.0, 2.0e6, 0.12),
        ("soft", 0.0, 1.0e7, 0.14),
        ("top", 128, 0.0, 0.24),
    ]

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    cur = a.copy()

    for mode, p, beta, fr in modes:
        phase_alloc = float(fr) * float(time_limit)
        remaining = float(time_limit) - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
        t_phase = min(phase_alloc, remaining)
        if t_phase < 5.0:
            continue
        phase_end = time.monotonic() + t_phase

        if mode == "lp":
            base_alpha = 0.14
        elif mode == "soft":
            base_alpha = 0.07
        else:
            base_alpha = 0.10
        no_acc = 0

        while time.monotonic() < phase_end:
            c, A = _autocorr_sym(cur, L)
            exact = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
            if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < best_score:
                best_score = exact
                best_a = cur.copy()

            val_cur = _objective_from_c(c, mode, int(p), float(beta))
            if not np.isfinite(val_cur):
                break

            w, S = _weights_from_c(c, mode, int(p), float(beta), use_sym)
            if S <= 1e-300:
                break

            W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
            C = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A) * W, L)[:n]
            C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
            C = np.nan_to_num(C, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

            ref = max(S, 1e-300)
            logr = np.log(np.maximum(C, 1e-300) / ref)
            logr = np.clip(logr, -4.0, 4.0)

            alphas = (base_alpha, 0.5 * base_alpha, 0.25 * base_alpha, 0.125 * base_alpha)
            best_trial = None
            best_val = val_cur
            best_exact_trial = exact

            for al in alphas:
                trial = cur * np.exp(-al * logr)
                if use_sym:
                    trial = _symmetrize_seq(trial)
                trial = _normalize_nonneg(trial)
                if trial is None:
                    continue
                c2 = _c_from_a(trial, L)
                val2 = _objective_from_c(c2, mode, int(p), float(beta))
                exact2 = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c2))
                if not np.isfinite(val2) or not np.isfinite(exact2):
                    continue
                if (
                    val2 < best_val - 1e-18
                    or (abs(val2 - best_val) <= 1e-18 and exact2 < best_exact_trial - 1e-15)
                ):
                    best_val = val2
                    best_exact_trial = exact2
                    best_trial = trial

            if best_trial is not None:
                cur = best_trial
                base_alpha = min(1.0, base_alpha * 1.08)
                no_acc = 0
            else:
                base_alpha = max(0.01, base_alpha * 0.70)
                no_acc += 1
                if no_acc > 250:
                    break

        sc_cur = _fast_exact_score(cur)
        if np.isfinite(sc_cur) and sc_cur < best_score:
            best_score = sc_cur
            best_a = cur.copy()

        # If this mode drifted badly in exact score, fall back to the best exact sequence.
        if sc_cur > best_score * (1.0 + 1e-3) and best_score < float("inf"):
            cur = best_a.copy()
        else:
            cur = cur.copy()

        if time.monotonic() - t_start > float(time_limit) - 2.0:
            break

    return best_a, best_score


def coordinate_polish(a, time_limit, use_sym):
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a is None or time_limit < 5.0:
        return a, float("inf")
    if use_sym:
        a = _normalize_nonneg(_symmetrize_seq(a))
        if a is None:
            return None, float("inf")

    n = int(a.size)
    L = _next_fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _fast_exact_score(a)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(2024)
    stall = 0
    t_start = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() - t_start < time_limit and stall < 70:
        c = _c_from_a(a, L)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        exact = float(2.0 * n * M)
        if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < best_score:
            best_score = exact
            best_a = a.copy()

        K = min(48, int(c.size))
        if K >= int(c.size):
            acts = np.arange(int(c.size))
        else:
            acts = np.argpartition(c, int(c.size) - K)[int(c.size) - K:]
        acts_int = [int(k) for k in acts]

        pot = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in acts_int:
            st = max(0, k - n + 1)
            en = min(n - 1, k)
            if st > en:
                continue
            idx = np.arange(st, en + 1)
            j = k - idx
            pot[idx] += a[j]
            pot[j] += a[idx]

        score = a * pot
        score = np.nan_to_num(score, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

        n_src = min(10, n)
        n_dst = min(30, n)
        if n_src >= n:
            src_idx = np.arange(n)
        else:
            src_idx = np.argpartition(-score, n - n_src)[n - n_src:]
        if n_dst >= n:
            dst_idx = np.arange(n)
        else:
            dst_idx = np.argsort(score)[:n_dst]

        pairs = []
        for s in src_idx:
            s = int(s)
            if a[s] <= 1e-12:
                continue
            for d in dst_idx:
                d = int(d)
                if d == s:
                    continue
                if use_sym and d == n - 1 - s:
                    continue
                red = score[s] - score[d]
                if red > 0.0:
                    pairs.append((float(red), s, d))
        pairs.sort(key=lambda z: z[0], reverse=True)

        frac_list = (0.01, 0.03, 0.06, 0.12, 0.25, 0.50)
        active_low_mans = np.array([c[k] for k in acts_int], dtype=np.float64)
        cand_list = []

        for _, s, d in pairs[:12]:
            s = int(s)
            d = int(d)
            for f in frac_list:
                delta = float(a[s] * f)
                if delta <= 0.0:
                    continue
                vals = active_low_mans.copy()
                local_ok = True
                d2 = 2 * d
                s2 = 2 * s
                sd = s + d
                for pos, k in enumerate(acts_int):
                    kd = k - d
                    ks = k - s
                    vi = float(a[kd]) if 0 <= kd < n else 0.0
                    ui = float(a[ks]) if 0 <= ks < n else 0.0
                    lin = 2.0 * delta * (vi - ui)
                    q = 0.0
                    if k == d2:
                        q += delta * delta
                    if k == s2:
                        q += delta * delta
                    if k == sd:
                        q -= 2.0 * delta * delta
                    vals[pos] += lin + q
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(vals)):
                    local_ok = False
                    break
                am = float(np.max(vals))
                if local_ok and np.isfinite(am):
                    cand_list.append((am, s, d, float(f)))

        if cand_list:
            cand_list.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
            improved = False
            seen = set()
            for _, s, d, f in cand_list[:5]:
                key = (s, d)
                if key in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(key)
                trial = a.copy()
                delta = float(trial[s] * f)
                if delta <= 0.0:
                    continue
                if delta > trial[s]:
                    delta = float(trial[s])
                trial[s] -= delta
                trial[d] += delta
                if use_sym:
                    trial = _symmetrize_seq(trial)
                trial = _normalize_nonneg(trial)
                if trial is None:
                    continue
                sc = _fast_exact_score(trial)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                    a = trial
                    best_a = trial.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                    stall = 0
                    improved = True
                    break

            if not improved and time.monotonic() - t_start < time_limit - 1.0:
                # A few random exploratory moves.
                for _ in range(3):
                    s = int(rng.integers(n))
                    d = int(rng.integers(n))
                    if s == d:
                        continue
                    trial = a.copy()
                    delta = float(trial[s] * 0.03)
                    if delta <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    trial[s] -= delta
                    trial[d] += delta
                    if use_sym:
                        trial = _symmetrize_seq(trial)
                    trial = _normalize_nonneg(trial)
                    if trial is None:
                        continue
                    sc = _fast_exact_score(trial)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                        a = trial
                        best_a = trial.copy()
                        best_score = sc
                        stall = 0
                        improved = True
                        break

            if not improved:
                stall += 1

        if time.monotonic() - t_start > time_limit - 1.0:
            break

    return best_a, best_score


def optimize_one(a, time_limit, use_sym, label=""):
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a is None or time_limit < 10.0:
        return None, float("inf")
    if use_sym:
        a = _normalize_nonneg(_symmetrize_seq(a))
        if a is None:
            return None, float("inf")

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _fast_exact_score(a)
    t_start = time.monotonic()

    a_m, sc_m = multiplicative_refine(best_a, float(time_limit) * 0.55, use_sym)
    if a_m is not None and np.isfinite(sc_m) and sc_m < best_score:
        best_a = a_m.copy()
        best_score = float(sc_m)

    rem = float(time_limit) - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    t_lpf = min(rem * 0.55, 420.0)
    if t_lpf > 20.0 and best_a.size <= 9500:
        a_l, sc_l = lbfgs_pipeline(best_a, t_lpf, use_sym)
        if a_l is not None and np.isfinite(sc_l) and sc_l < best_score:
            best_a = a_l.copy()
            best_score = float(sc_l)
        rem = float(time_limit) - (time.monotonic() - t_start)

    t_c = min(rem * 0.70, 140.0)
    if t_c > 10.0 and best_a.size <= 10000:
        # Use full coordinate moves even if the “best so far” is symmetric.
        a_c, sc_c = coordinate_polish(best_a, t_c, use_sym=False)
        if a_c is not None and np.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c < best_score:
            best_a = a_c.copy()
            best_score = float(sc_c)

    return best_a, best_score


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    global _LBFGS_BEST_A, _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    time_budget = min(float(budget_s) - 60.0, 1740.0)
    if time_budget < 90.0:
        time_budget = 90.0

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    _LBFGS_BEST_A = None
    _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE = float("inf")

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        if p.size > 0:
            pp = _normalize_nonneg(p)
            if pp is not None:
                parent = pp
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        parent = _default_sequence(6700)

    base_len = int(parent.size)

    best_seq, best_score = _update_best(best_seq, best_score, parent, "parent")
    best_seq, best_score = _update_best(
        best_seq, best_score, _symmetrize_seq(parent), "sym_parent"
    )

    # A few quick diverse starts to reduce the risk of a single basin.
    for seed, sig in [(1, 0.05), (2, 0.12), (3, 0.25)]:
        if time.monotonic() - t_global > time_budget * 0.05:
            break
        try:
            a = _noisy_smooth_start(parent, base_len, seed, sig)
            best_seq, best_score = _update_best(best_seq, best_score, a, "noise" + str(seed))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = parent.copy()
        best_score = _fast_exact_score(best_seq)

    # Lengths: main parent length, one medium, one larger but still inspectable.
    lengths = []
    for l in [base_len, base_len + 1000, base_len + 2500, 9000]:
        l = int(l)
        if 3000 <= l <= 9000 and l not in lengths:
            lengths.append(l)
    lengths = lengths[:3]

    alloc = [0.52, 0.30, 0.18]

    for i, ntry in enumerate(lengths):
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_global
        remaining_total = time_budget - elapsed
        if remaining_total < 90.0:
            break

        al = float(alloc[i]) if i < len(alloc) else 0.10
        t_len = min(al * time_budget, remaining_total * 0.96)
        if t_len < 70.0:
            continue

        if len(best_seq) == ntry:
            init_best = best_seq.copy()
        else:
            init_best = _interpolate_sequence(best_seq, ntry)

        init_parent = _interpolate_sequence(parent, ntry)
        init_parent_sym = _symmetrize_seq(init_parent)
        for cand in (init_best, init_parent, init_parent_sym):
            init_best, _ = _fake_update(init_best, _fast_exact_score(_normalize_nonneg(cand)) if cand is not None else float("inf"), cand)

        # Symmetric run.
        t_sym = min(t_len * (0.80 if i == 0 else 0.70), t_len - 20.0)
        if t_sym > 60.0:
            a_out, sc_out = optimize_one(init_best, t_sym, True, "sym_n" + str(ntry))
            best_seq, best_score = _update_best(best_seq, best_score, a_out, "sym_opt" + str(ntry))

        # Full run on the first length only, if there is time.
        if i == 0:
            elapsed_len = time.monotonic() - (t_global + (time.monotonic() - t_global) - (time.monotonic() - t_global))  # placeholder, use rem
            rem_len = t_len - min(t_len, time.monotonic() - t_global + 0.0)
            # Simpler: recompute remaining for this length allocation.
            time_spent_len = min(t_len, time.monotonic() - t_global - 0.0)
            rem_len = max(0.0, t_len - time_spent_len)
            t_full = min(rem_len * 0.55, 320.0)
            if t_full > 70.0 and ntry <= 8200:
                if len(best_seq) == ntry:
                    full_init = best_seq.copy()
                else:
                    full_init = _interpolate_sequence(best_seq, ntry)
                a_out, sc_out = optimize_one(full_init, t_full, False, "full_n" + str(ntry))
                best_seq, best_score = _update_best(best_seq, best_score, a_out, "full_opt" + str(ntry))

    # Final polish.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_global
    remaining = time_budget - elapsed
    if remaining > 90.0 and best_seq is not None:
        a_c, sc_c = coordinate_polish(best_seq, min(remaining * 0.55, 160.0), False)
        best_seq, best_score = _update_best(best_seq, best_score, a_c, "final_coord")

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_global
    remaining = time_budget - elapsed
    if remaining > 120.0 and best_seq is not None:
        a_l, sc_l = lbfgs_pipeline(best_seq, min(remaining * 0.55, 260.0), False)
        best_seq, best_score = _update_best(best_seq, best_score, a_l, "final_lbfgs")

    # Final direct validation / fallback.
    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = parent.copy()
        best_score = _fast_exact_score(best_seq)

    try:
        final_list = [float(x) for x in best_seq]
        direct = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        if np.isfinite(direct):
            # If direct objective is slightly better (possible due to different summation), keep sequence.
            if direct < best_score:
                best_score = float(direct)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_seq is None or best_seq.size == 0:
        best_seq = _default_sequence(6700)

    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]


def _fake_update(best_seq, best_score, cand):
    if cand is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    cand = _normalize_nonneg(cand)
    if cand is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    sc = _fast_exact_score(cand)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        return cand.copy(), sc
    return best_seq, best_score
```
